# moderated

OpenAI `omni-moderation-latest` text-only run, 2026-11-19. 835 text-bearing posts scored.

Part of the [ModerationBench results](../README.md). See [metrics.md](metrics.md) for pooled numbers and the method, and [comparison.md](../comparison.md) for the Flagline side-by-side.

## Metrics

| Precision | Recall | F1 | Accuracy | Flag rate | n |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0.7985 | 0.4601 | 0.5838 | 0.4587 | 47.5% | 835 |

Unsafe is the positive class. OpenAI's `flagged` is the provider's own binary decision; there is no separate flag/block distinction.

`Predicted` maps OpenAI's `flagged` decision to Unsafe and its decided category to a Bluesky code. `Decided category` is the highest-scoring category whose boolean is true, or `-` when the item is not flagged. `Confidence` is that category's score when flagged, and `1 - top score` when not flagged. `Truth` shows the first annotated category when a post has several; [metrics.md](metrics.md) counts every annotated category.

## Confusion

|  | Actual Safe | Actual Unsafe |
|---|---|---|
| Predicted Safe | 66 | 372 |
| Predicted Unsafe | 80 | 317 |

## Decisions

| not_flagged | flagged | Top OpenAI categories on flagged rows |
|---|---|---|
| 438 | 397 | harassment 228, violence 78, sexual 60, self-harm 13, self-harm/intent 5, hate 5, harassment/threatening 5, sexual/minors 1, self-harm/instructions 1, hate/threatening 1 |

## Media

| Text only | n | With media | n |
|---|---|---|---|
| 68.6% | 347 | 29.7% | 488 |

Media means the post had an image or video. OpenAI's API can accept images, but this run sent text only.

False negatives: expected Unsafe, predicted Safe (372).
